TL;DR: 蛋白质语言模型在高度不平衡的数据集上训练，导致对欠代表序列（如病毒蛋白）的表征存在偏见。研究发现嵌入空间中存在一个主导的"自然性"轴，该轴与掩码重建困惑度对齐，将序列从细胞蛋白到病毒蛋白再到乱序和随机序列排序。模型规模扩大会不均匀地压缩不同病毒家族的该轴。尽管如此，嵌入仍保留病毒特异性信号，病毒蛋白在零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征之外仍保持线性可分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究蛋白质语言模型如何在高度不平衡的训练数据下表征欠代表的生物序列（以病毒蛋白为例），理解模型对自然序列与非自然序列的区分机制及其背后的结构组织原则。

Method: 以病毒蛋白作为欠代表案例，跨ESM模型家族进行分析。通过识别嵌入空间中的"自然性"轴、测量掩码重建困惑度、分析模型规模扩大的影响，并评估零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征之外的线性可分性。

Result: (1) 嵌入空间中存在一个主导的"自然性"轴，按细胞蛋白→病毒蛋白→乱序→随机序列排序；(2) 模型规模扩大会不均匀地压缩不同病毒家族的该轴；(3) 嵌入仍保留病毒特异性信号，病毒蛋白在零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征之上仍保持线性可分。

Conclusion: 蛋白质语言模型的表征由一种广义的"自然性"概念结构化，同时保留了不同生物群组特有的信息，为理解模型对不平衡数据的学习机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Protein language models are trained on highly imbalanced datasets, raising the question of how they represent underrepresented biological sequences. Using viral proteins as a case study across ESM model families, we identify a dominant nativeness axis in embedding space, aligned with masked reconstruction perplexity, that orders sequences from well-modeled cellular proteins through viral proteins to shuffled and random sequences. Scaling contracts this axis unevenly across viral families. Despite this, protein language model embeddings retain viral-specific signal: viral proteins remain linearly separable beyond zero-shot perplexity and shallow sequence features. Together, these results suggest that pLM representations are structured by a general notion of nativeness while preserving information specific to distinct biological groups.

</details>


### [250] [The Mathematics of AI Winters: The mathematical Taxonomy of Paradigm Fragility in AI Winter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12610)
*Miquel Noguer i Alonso,David Pacheco Aznar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出补充性论点，认为AI冬季不仅源于工程失败和商业失望，还受到形式化数学瓶颈的制约，包括感知机不可能结果、神经网络训练的计算复杂性、高维非参数估计的极小化最大速率、梯度消失问题以及统计学习理论等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的对AI冬季的解释主要聚焦于工程失败、商业失望和过度预期，本文旨在补充另一种视角：主导范式所遇到的形式化数学瓶颈也是导致AI冬季的重要因素。

Method: 通过综合分析和回顾感知机不可能结果、神经网络训练的计算复杂性、高维估计的统计极限、梯度消失分析以及统计学习理论等经典数学瓶颈，并将其与AI领域的重大失望事件进行对应。

Result: 展示了早期AI的若干核心失望事件与精确的数学瓶颈之间存在对齐关系，并指出后来的突破性进展只是缓解而非消除了这些瓶颈。

Conclusion: AI冬季的原因不仅包括工程和商业因素，还包含深层的形式化数学限制；后续的突破虽缓解了这些问题，但并未从根本上消除它们。

Abstract: Two major periods of reduced funding and confidence in artificial intelligence research, commonly called the first and second AI winters, are usually explained through engineering failure, commercial disappointment, and inflated expectations. This article develops a complementary thesis: that the dominant paradigms of those periods also met genuine formal barriers, including limitations of representation, optimisation, computational complexity, statistical learnability, and high-dimensional approximation. The contribution is synthetic rather than archival. We do not claim that particular theorems mechanically caused the winters; rather, we show that several central disappointments of early AI were aligned with mathematically precise bottlenecks. We analyse these bottlenecks through the perceptron impossibility results of Minsky and Papert, the complexity-theoretic hardness of exact neural-network training established by Blum and Rivest, minimax rates for nonparametric estimation in high dimension due to Stone, vanishing-gradient analyses by Hochreiter and by Bengio and collaborators, and classical statistical learning theory in the tradition of Vapnik and Chervonenkis, Valiant, and Blumer and collaborators. We then relate these barriers to the later breakthroughs that mitigated, rather than eliminated, them.

</details>


### [251] [Evaluation of AutoML Frameworks for IDS under Imbalanced Data Conditions of the NSL-KDD Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12611)
*Wiliane Carolina Silva,Evandro César Vilas Boas,Felipe A. P. de Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究了严重类别不平衡对基于NSL-KDD数据集的多类网络入侵检测自动机器学习（AutoML）框架性能的影响，发现集成学习和不平衡感知优化能提升少数类检测能力，其中PyCaret以66%的宏F1得分表现最佳，揭示了仅依赖准确率优化的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多通过二分类或移除少数类来简化问题，缺乏对真实多类不平衡场景下AutoML框架性能的系统评估，亟需建立标准化基准。

Method: 保留原始五类分布（包括R2L、U2R等高度稀疏攻击），在统一可复现协议下分析九种开源AutoML框架的架构设计、集成策略、验证流程、超参数优化及不平衡处理机制。

Result: 集成学习与不平衡感知优化的框架表现更优；PyCaret以66%宏F1领先，AutoGluon以55%次之；缺乏原生平衡支持的框架在少数类检测上显著退化；高加权指标可能掩盖对稀有攻击类别的泛化不足。

Conclusion: 建立了多类不平衡入侵检测的AutoML标准化基准，揭示了当前架构缺陷，提出需原生集成不平衡感知优化、重采样与分层评估策略。源代码已公开。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of severe class imbalance on the performance of automated machine learning (AutoML) frameworks for multiclass network intrusion detection using the NSL-KDD dataset. Unlike previous studies that simplify the problem through binary classification or minority-class removal, we preserve the original five-class distribution, including highly underrepresented attacks such as R2L and U2R, enabling a realistic evaluation of imbalance-sensitive learning behavior. Nine open-source AutoML frameworks were analyzed under a unified and reproducible experimental protocol, considering differences in architectural design, ensemble strategies, validation procedures, hyperparameter optimization, and imbalance-handling mechanisms. The results demonstrate that frameworks incorporating ensemble learning and imbalance-aware optimization achieve better minority-class discrimination. PyCaret obtained the best overall performance, reaching 66\% macro-F1, followed by AutoGluon with 55\%, whereas frameworks lacking native balancing support exhibited significant degradation in minority-class detection capability. The analysis further shows that accuracy-oriented optimization alone is insufficient for highly imbalanced IDS scenarios, since high-weighted metrics may coexist with poor generalization on rare attack categories. As a contribution, this work establishes a standardized benchmark for AutoML-based intrusion detection under severe multiclass imbalance, highlighting current architectural limitations and the need for native integration of imbalance-aware optimization, resampling, and stratified evaluation strategies into automated learning pipelines. The source code is publicly available.

</details>


### [252] [Towards Provably Fair Machine Learning: Bayesian Approaches For Consistent and Transparent Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12615)
*Owen O'Neill,Fintan Costello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过定义确定性和统计一致性两个约束条件，本文提出了公平贝叶斯分类器（Fair Bayesian classifier），该分类器在所有子群中同时强制执行这两个约束，并在无法做出一致确定性预测时弃权。在三个基准数据集上，该分类器实现了零一致性误差，同时超过了基线准确率和多重校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML分类器在高风险领域中对细粒度子群的预测质量参差不齐，特别是在多个特征交叉定义的子群中，预测往往与观测数据不一致。正则化加剧了这一问题，因为它通过将小子群合并到较大群体中来提升整体性能，从而不成比例地影响少数群体。

Method: 研究者首先定义了预测一致性的两个要求：确定性和统计一致性。确定性要求相同的个体获得相同的预测；统计一致性要求在显著性水平α下，无法拒绝子群预测来自贝叶斯最优目标分布的假设。由此推导出公平贝叶斯分类器，该分类器同时在每个群体和子群中强制执行这两种一致性，并在无法做出确定性预测时弃权。

Result: 在Adult、COMPAS和Bank Marketing三个基准数据集上，标准分类器对相当比例的子群产生了统计上不一致的预测。公平贝叶斯分类器通过构造实现了零一致性误差，同时在每个测试数据集上超过了基线准确率和多重校准性能。

Conclusion: 统计一致性为预测质量提供了原则性基础，对算法公平性有直接启示。少数群体不成比例地集中在小子群中，而恰好在这些子群中频率论推断最不可靠；解决这一推断问题是迈向公平ML的必要步骤。通过在最精细的数据支持分辨率上强制执行贝叶斯一致性，该分类器证明了具有原则性弃权的详尽子群公平性在实践中是可以实现的。

Abstract: ML classifiers deployed in high-stakes domains produce predictions whose quality varies systematically across subgroups. For granular subgroups defined by intersections of multiple features, predictions are often inconsistent with the observed data: the model's outputs contradict the evidence available for that subgroup. This problem is exacerbated by regularisation, which improves aggregate performance by collapsing small subgroups into larger groups, disproportionately affecting demographic minorities. We define two requirements for consistent prediction: determinism (identical individuals receive identical predictions) and statistical consistency (we cannot reject, at significance level alpha, the hypothesis that the predictions for a subgroup were drawn from the Bayesian optimal target distribution inferred for that subgroup). From these requirements we derive the Fair Bayesian classifier, which enforces both across every group and subgroup simultaneously and abstains whenever no consistent deterministic prediction is possible. On three benchmark datasets (Adult, COMPAS, and Bank Marketing), standard classifiers produce statistically inconsistent predictions for a substantial proportion of subgroups. Our classifier achieves zero consistency error by construction while exceeding baseline accuracy and multicalibration on every dataset tested. Statistical consistency provides a principled foundation for prediction quality with direct implications for algorithmic fairness. Minority demographics are disproportionately concentrated in small subgroups, precisely where frequentist inference is least reliable; addressing this inference problem is therefore a necessary step toward fair ML. By enforcing Bayesian consistency at the finest resolution the data supports, the our classifier demonstrates that exhaustive subgroup fairness with principled abstention is achievable in practice.

</details>


### [253] [Bag of Dims: Training-Free Mechanistic Interpretability via Dimension-Level Sign Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12629)
*Varun Reddy Nalagatla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Transformer隐藏状态的标准基（standard basis）本身就是一个无需训练、架构通用的特征基。每个维度通过符号编码语义内容、通过幅度编码置信度，作为独立的二元寄存器。实验证明，仅利用符号模式即可达到较高预测准确率，且各维度之间几乎不存在交叉结构。这一发现贯穿整个Transformer计算路径（包括注意力K/V投影和前馈网络），无需额外训练或优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer隐藏状态的标准基是否已经可以作为有效的特征表示，而无需使用训练得到的探针或复杂变换。作者质疑了‘特征隐藏在隐藏状态的线性变换中’这一常见假设，试图证明标准基本身就具有语义含义。

Method: 使用四个递进实验验证：1) 符号模式预测任务（保留符号、统一幅度）；2) 基于单token类型缓存的无监督类别发现；3) 训练探针验证维度正交性；4) 扩展到注意力层和前馈网络的分析。覆盖Qwen 3.5-4B、Gemma 3-4B、Mistral 7B三个模型家族。

Result: 1) 仅符号模式通过LM头达到72-93% top-5准确率；纯汉明评分达80-90% top-4096。2) 无监督发现175个类别（平均AUC 0.80）。3) 训练探针仅+0.018 AUC增益且收敛于轴对齐权重。4) 175个类别在注意力K/V投影中仍可发现。5) 20%特征关联到单个写入神经元，99.9%原型可通过top-200神经元多数投票达到>0.70一致率。6) 完全无监督发现扩展到1500个特征（100%产出率，99%稀疏度），互信息仅0.0014比特。

Conclusion: 标准基在整个Transformer计算路径中已足够用于特征读取，无需训练、优化或大量计算资源（仅需每个词汇token一次前向传播）。这为可解释性研究提供了更简单、更高效的框架。

Abstract: We show that the standard basis of transformer hidden states already provides a training-free, architecture-general feature basis. Individual dimensions encode semantic content via their signs and confidence via their magnitudes, functioning as independent binary registers. We validate this Bag of Dims framework across three model families (Qwen 3.5-4B, Gemma 3-4B, Mistral 7B) through four progressive experiments. Sign patterns alone carry predictive content: replacing all magnitudes with unity achieves 72-93% top-5 next-token accuracy through the LM head, and pure Hamming scoring without any decoder reaches 80-90% top-4096. These sign patterns organize into semantic features: using a single-token type cache (one forward pass per vocabulary token, no context), we discover 175 categories via per-dimension sign consistency (mean AUC 0.80) from 50 anchors with zero training. A trained probe adds only +0.018 AUC and converges to axis-aligned weights, confirming negligible cross-dimension structure. This structure extends to attention: all 175 categories remain discoverable in K and V projections. On the write side, static FFN weight inspection links 20% of features to individual writer neurons (>0.70 agreement; random controls: 0%), with top-200 neuron coalitions achieving >0.70 agreement on 99.9% of prototypes via majority vote. Fully unsupervised discovery (random seeds, no labels) scales to 1500 features at 100% yield and 99% sparsity across all three models, with pairwise MI of 0.0014 bits confirming low inter-dimension coupling. These results establish that the standard basis already suffices for feature reading throughout the transformer compute pathway, requiring no training, no optimization, and no GPU-days beyond a single forward pass per vocabulary token.

</details>


### [254] [Keep Policy Gradient in Charge: Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12634)
*Tianyu Ding,Jianhong Xin,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SGCD（兄弟引导信用蒸馏），用于长视野工具使用强化学习中的信用分配，通过对比成功与失败的兄弟轨迹并利用外部LLM生成步骤级信用参考，解决了直接标记级自我蒸馏会放大有用技能和有害捷径的问题，在AppWorld和τ³-airline基准上超过了GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野工具使用强化学习仅从结果验证中学习，但轨迹级优势被广播到大量推理、API和回答标记中。直接标记级自我蒸馏会无声地破坏工具使用：它模仿教师行为而不了解验证器奖励哪些动作，导致有用技能和有害捷径被一起放大。

Method: SGCD使用蒸馏进行信用分配而非作为竞争性的演员损失。动态采样产生混合的成功和失败兄弟轨迹；外部LLM总结它们的对比形成仅用于训练的步骤级信用参考；密集的教师/学生散度驱动信用重新分配；有界的分离信用权重重塑GRPO标记优势。部署的学生模型看不到外部LLM、兄弟证据或预言机。

Result: 在AppWorld上，test_normal的TGC从42.9提升到45.6，test_challenge从24.7提升到27.0；在τ³-airline上，pass@1从0.583提升到0.602。

Conclusion: SGCD通过利用蒸馏进行信用分配，有效地解决了长视野工具使用强化学习中直接自我蒸馏的问题，在多个基准上取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-use reinforcement learning can learn from outcome verification, but its
  trajectory-level advantage is broadcast across many reasoning, API, and answer tokens.
  Self-distillation promises a denser signal by reusing a policy's own rollouts or a privileged
  teacher. We show, however, that direct token-level self-distillation can silently destroy tool use:
  it rehearses teacher behavior without knowing which actions the verifier rewards, so useful skills
  and harmful shortcuts are amplified together. We introduce Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation
  (SGCD), which uses distillation for credit assignment rather than as a competing actor loss.
  Dynamic sampling produces mixed successful and failed sibling rollouts; an external LLM summarizes
  their contrast into a training-only stepwise credit reference; dense teacher/student divergence
  drives credit reassignment; and bounded detached credit weights reshape GRPO token advantages. The
  deployed student sees no external LLM, sibling evidence, or oracle. Across AppWorld and
  $τ^3$-airline, SGCD improves over matched GRPO comparators: AppWorld TGC $42.9 \to 45.6$ on
  test_normal and $24.7 \to 27.0$ on test_challenge, and $τ^3$-airline pass@1 $0.583 \to 0.602$.

</details>


### [255] [The Metric Picks the Winner: Evaluation Choice Flips Model Rankings for Drug-Response Prediction in Unseen Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12639)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Riya Bisht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在VCPI预测竞赛的THP-1 DRUG-seq数据上，系统性地比较了从简单基线到深度模型的多种方法。结果表明，评价指标的选择会完全逆转模型排名：在简化的基因方差加权代理指标下，线性回归胜出；但在竞赛官方的活性化合物加权指标（wMSE）下，深度融合模型显著优于线性基线和检索方法。这是首次在真实药物化学数据上证明"指标决定胜者"的度量校准效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测未见过药物对细胞转录组的影响是计算细胞生物学的核心难题。近期基准测试表明，一旦测试化合物按化学结构严格分离，复杂模型往往无法击败简单基线。该研究的出发点正是系统检验这一现象，并探索不同评价指标对模型排名的影响。

Method: 提出了一个分阶段的方法框架：1）简单基线（未处理对照和训练集平均响应）；2）非参数检索（基于Tanimoto系数的加权平均）；3）融合阶段——将冻结的化学嵌入与检索支持特征结合，预测相对于均值的残差，并包含不确定性头和基因程序模块。在Bemis-Murcko骨架切分下，使用14,026个训练化合物进行实验。

Result: 结果揭示了一个关键的度量效应：在简化的逆方差加权代理指标下，基于Morgan指纹的线性回归表现最佳，胜过了深度模型、检索方法和ChemBERTa；但在竞赛官方的真实活性化合物加权指标（Mejia权重，wMSE）下，排名完全反转，深度融合解码器显著优于线性指纹基线（wMSE降低0.012，配对bootstrap p<10^-4）。

Conclusion: 该研究首次在真实药物化学数据上证明了度量校准效应——评价指标的选择直接决定了哪个模型是"胜者"。这一发现此前仅在遗传扰动数据中得到验证。研究还发布了一个可复现的流程，连接到官方评分器，可在真实的1064×12,995网格上生成有效提交。

Abstract: Predicting how a cell's transcriptome responds to a drug it has never seen is a core, hard problem in computational cell biology: recent benchmarks show complex models often fail to beat trivial baselines once test compounds are held out by chemistry. We study one cell line and assay, THP-1 cells profiled by DRUG-seq, scored by the active-compound weighted MSE(wMSE) of the VCPI prediction contest. We propose a staged approach: dumb baselines (untreated control and mean training-compound response) that the field keeps failing to beat; non-parametric retrieval (a Tanimoto-weighted average of a held-out compound's nearest training compounds); and a fusion stage combining a frozen chemistry embedding with retrieval-support features to predict the residual over the mean, with an uncertainty head and gene programs. On the released VCPI THP-1 drug-seq data (14,026 training compounds), under a Bemis-Murcko scaffold split, the model ranking inverts depending on the metric. Under an inverse-variance per-gene proxy, a regularized linear regression on Morgan fingerprints appears to win over the deep models, retrieval, and ChemBERTa -- the textbook "simple baselines win" result. But under the contest's true active-set metric (per-(gene, compound) Mejia weights, validated against the official scorer; mean baseline 0.535 vs the organizers' 0.507 reference), that reverses: the deep models win, our fusion decoder significantly beats the linear fingerprint baseline (-0.012 wMSE, paired bootstrap p < 10^-4), and the proxy's winner becomes the worst chemistry-aware predictor. Picking the metric picks the winner -- to our knowledge the first demonstration on real held-out drug chemistry of the metric-calibration effect established largely on genetic perturbation. We release a reproducible pipeline
  wired to the official scorer that emits a valid submission over the real 1064 x 12,995 grid.

</details>


### [256] [Individual Control Barrier Functions-Guided Diffusion Model for Safe Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12640)
*Qingyun Guo,Junyi Shi,Jianuo Huang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种将神经个体控制屏障函数嵌入扩散模型的离线多智能体强化学习算法，在轨迹生成中增强安全性，并通过逆动力学恢复控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习中的扩散模型方法主要关注单智能体场景，未能解决多智能体环境下的安全性挑战。

Method: 将神经个体控制屏障函数（neural individual control barrier functions）嵌入扩散模型，在轨迹生成过程中增强安全性，并通过逆动力学（inverse dynamics）恢复控制策略。

Result: 在多个基准测试中显著提升了安全性，同时保持了具有竞争力的奖励表现。

Conclusion: 该算法有效弥补了扩散模型在多智能体离线强化学习中安全性的空白，实现了安全性与奖励之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning allows control policies to be learned directly from data without online interaction, making it suitable for safety-critical tasks. Recent studies have applied diffusion models to offline reinforcement learning to leverage their strong capacity for modeling complex data distributions. However, existing approaches primarily focus on single-agent settings, leaving the safety challenges in multi-agent environments largely unexplored. In this work, we propose a safe offline multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm that embeds neural individual control barrier functions into the diffusion model to enhance safety during trajectory generation, with control policies recovered through inverse dynamics. We evaluate our algorithm across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating substantial safety improvements while maintaining competitive rewards.

</details>


### [257] [TEDD: Robust Detection of Unstable Temporal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12643)
*Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Bruno Casal Laraña,Nádia Soares,Miguel Araújo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TEDD方法，通过回归模型预测实例时间戳来检测特征分布随时间的变化，从而识别可能导致机器学习模型不稳定的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时序数据中特征分布会随时间变化，导致机器学习模型性能快速下降，因此需要自动识别随时间变化的特征以采取缓解措施。

Method: 利用回归模型预测实例的时间戳，通过评估各特征对时间戳预测的贡献程度，来检测哪些特征发生了分布漂移。

Result: 该方法能检测所有类型的基本变化（数值型和类别型）、多变量漂移；返回可比较的变化量值；无需参数调优；在特征数量和实例数量上均具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: TEDD是一种无需参数调优、可扩展且能全面检测各类特征分布漂移的有效方法，有助于构建更鲁棒的机器学习模型。

Abstract: When working with real-world temporal data, it is common to encounter features whose distribution is changing over time. The naive employment of Machine Learning models on this unstable data might lead to rapidly degrading performance, especially if the new distribution is much different from what was previously seen during training. In order to cope with this problem, it is critical to automatically identify features that are changing over time. With these features detected, data scientists and other practitioners will be able to mitigate the issue (for instance, by applying data transformations), deploying more robust models that retain high performance for longer periods of time. In this paper, we describe which temporal changes a feature should not suffer from, and propose TEDD, a technique to a) identify when a dataset might lead to an unstable Machine Learning model and b) automatically detect which features cause such lack of robustness. In order to achieve it, we leverage a regression model to highlight which features contribute to a good prediction of an instance's timestamp. We compare our approach to other methods in real and synthetic data, testing their detection capability on all simple change patterns. We show that our method: detects all types of basic changes, both for numerical and categorical features; can detect multivariate drifts; returns a comparable value measuring the amount of change of each feature; requires no parameter tuning; and is scalable both on number of features and instances of the dataset.

</details>


### [258] [Physics-Aware Auxiliary Losses Improve Out-of-Distribution Generalization of a GNN Synthesizability Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12651)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在生成式药物发现中，利用廉价物理先验（拓扑复杂度与应变能）作为辅助监督信号，提升图神经网络在分布外（OOD）数据上的合成可及性过滤器性能。在65K分子数据集上的多种子实验表明，物理先验带来统计显著的OOD提升（AUC +0.0066），但效果温和；且单种子实验会得出误导性结论，需多种子验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成可及性过滤器（如SAScore、SCScore、RAscore、DeepSA）完全基于统计学习，在生成模型提出的分布外分子上性能严重退化。作者希望探索廉价、闭式的物理先验（而非复杂计算）能否作为辅助监督信号，提升图神经网络在OOD场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 在GINE图神经网络骨干上增加两种辅助损失：1）拓扑复杂度回归（以Bertz指数为监督信号）；2）应变能软惩罚（以MMFF94力场能量为监督信号）。使用65,177分子数据集（HIV、Tox21、COCONUT），以SAScore阈值标注。采用单源OOD划分（训练集为药物类HIV+Tox21，测试集为天然产物COCONUT），进行4路消融实验（基线/+复杂度/+应变/+两者），重复5个随机种子，使用配对bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 所有三个物理感知变体均比基线（平均OOD AUC 0.9774）有统计显著提升：+复杂度Δ=+0.0060（95% CI [+0.0023, +0.0102]）、+应变Δ=+0.0032（[+0.0008, +0.0052]）、+两者Δ=+0.0066（[+0.0038, +0.0093]），所有置信区间不包含零，组合效果最佳。但效果温和，且在分布内性能无差异。重要方法学发现：单种子实验会得出定性不同（非单调）的结论，多种子验证才可靠。

Conclusion: 廉价的物理先验（拓扑复杂度和应变能）可作为有效的辅助监督信号，在分布外场景下温和但统计显著地提升合成可及性过滤器的泛化能力；同时强调了多种子实验验证在OOD评估中的必要性，避免单次实验的误导性结论。

Abstract: Machine-learning drug-discovery pipelines increasingly rely on generative models that propose molecules far from the data used to train downstream synthesizability filters. Existing
  filters (SAScore, SCScore, RAscore, DeepSA) are purely statistical and degrade in exactly this out-of-distribution (OOD) regime. We ask whether cheap, closed-form physical priors, used
  as auxiliary supervision on a graph neural network (GNN), improve OOD generalization. We add two auxiliary losses to a GINE backbone: a topological complexity regression supervised by
  the Bertz index, and a strain-energy soft penalty supervised by MMFF94 force-field energy. On a 65,177-molecule corpus (HIV, Tox21, COCONUT) labeled by SAScore thresholds we reproduce
  a strong in-distribution baseline, then evaluate a 4-way ablation (baseline / +complexity / +strain / +both) on a single-source OOD split (train on drug-like HIV+Tox21, test on
  COCONUT natural products), repeated over 5 seeds with paired bootstrap confidence intervals. All three physics-aware variants give a small but statistically significant OOD improvement
  over the baseline (mean OOD AUC 0.9774): +complexity Delta = +0.0060 (95% CI [+0.0023, +0.0102]), +strain Delta = +0.0032 ([+0.0008, +0.0052]), +both Delta = +0.0066 ([+0.0038,
  +0.0093]); every interval excludes zero, and the combination is best. The variants are indistinguishable in-distribution, so the effect is visible only under OOD evaluation. We are
  explicit that the effects are modest, and we report a cautionary methodological finding: a single-seed version of this experiment produced a qualitatively different (non-monotone)
  story that did not survive multi-seed evaluation.

</details>


### [259] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基准测试了物理信息神经网络（PINN）在化疗药物动力学（PK）部分观测问题上的表现。在线性二室模型中，PINN匹配了标准临床基线（NLS）的精度，同时一次训练即可输出组织浓度曲线；在Michaelis-Menten扩展模型中，NLS因闭式解缺失而失效，而PINN揭示了模型不可识别性问题。PINN并非超越NLS，而是提供了一个统一框架：在标准问题上匹配教科书估计器，暴露其隐藏的结构可识别性问题，并能整合异构测量数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物学中许多问题仅有部分状态可观测（如药物动力学中血浆浓度可测、组织浓度不可测），需要一种既能利用已知动力学方程又能处理部分观测数据的方法。标准临床方法NLS依赖于解析闭式解，在非线性动力学（如Michaelis-Menten）中不再适用，且不能估计不可观测状态。

Method: 使用PINN（物理信息神经网络）在化疗药物动力学二室模型上进行基准测试，与标准临床基线NLS（非线性最小二乘解析双指数血浆解）和纯数据驱动的MLP基线进行比较。在两个问题（线性二室和Michaelis-Menten扩展）上评估。

Result: 线性二室：PINN匹配NLS精度（因子小常数内），同时单次训练输出组织曲线，MLP在组织上误差~10x。Michaelis-Menten：NLS因闭式解缺失返回无意义参数；PINN揭示模型不可识别（k12→0）。添加两个稀疏组织观测后：PINN恢复k21精度1%，Vmax、Km在一个标准差内，k12从0.02→0.82但~2σ低于真值。

Conclusion: PINN并非要击败NLS，而是提供一个统一配方：在教科书问题上匹配标准估计器，暴露教科书估计器隐藏的结构可识别性问题，并将异构测量整合到单一损失函数中。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [260] [A Zero-shot Generalized Graph Anomaly Detection Framework via Node Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12673)
*Phan Nguyen,Dat Cao,Hien Chu,Khue Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlignGAD是一个零样本泛化图异常检测框架，通过全局统一模块对齐异构特征与谱域信号归一化、聚类模块捕获群组级异常模式、节点差异评分模块聚合多视图异常证据，实现跨域零样本异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域图异常检测方法过度依赖特定数据集的语义特征和结构模式，导致泛化能力受限，无法有效适应异构图数据中的未知目标域。

Method: 提出三组件框架：1) 全局统一模块对齐异构节点特征并在谱域归一化图信号；2) 聚类模块构建聚类感知图视图以捕获群组级异常模式；3) 节点差异评分模块衡量重构差异并聚合不同图视图的异常证据。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的零样本图异常检测设置下验证了AlignGAD的有效性。

Conclusion: AlignGAD通过特征对齐、聚类感知视图和差异评分聚合，有效解决了跨域图异常检测的泛化问题，在零样本场景下表现出色。

Abstract: Cross-domain graph anomaly detection (GAD) aims to identify abnormal nodes in unseen target graphs, showing strong potential in real-world applications with heterogeneous graph data. However, existing methods often depend on dataset-specific feature semantics and structural patterns, which limits their ability to generalize across different domains. To address this challenge, we propose AlignGAD, a zero-shot generalized graph anomaly detection framework. Our framework is built upon three key components: a Global Unification Module that aligns heterogeneous node features and normalizes graph signals in the spectral domain; a Clustering Module that constructs cluster-aware graph views to capture group-level abnormal patterns; and a Node Discrepancy Scoring Module that measures reconstruction discrepancy and aggregates anomaly evidence from different graph views. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AlignGAD under the zero-shot GAD setting.

</details>


### [261] [Fed-FBD: Federated Functional Block Diversification for Isolation, Privacy, and Surgical Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12679)
*Weijie Chen,Alan B. McMillan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-FBD 是一种模块化联邦学习架构，通过将 ResNet 分解为六个功能块并维护贡献者标记的块仓库，实现了块级隔离、隐私设计和无需重训的机器遗忘，在多个数据集上以少量精度损失换取了对抗鲁棒性和隐私保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习（如 FedAvg）将每个客户端视为黑箱，无法隔离恶意贡献者、审计单个客户端影响或实现已退出参与者的被遗忘权，缺乏细粒度的安全与隐私保障机制。

Method: Fed-FBD 将 ResNet 骨干分解为 stem、四个残差组和分类头六个功能块，维护一个包含 N 种颜色变体的仓库，每颜色由独立追踪和贡献者标记的块组装而成。架构上实现了块级隔离，确保恶意客户端无法污染干净颜色；设计上提供隐私保障（成员推断优势在应用隐私机制前已不可区分）；并通过手术式机器遗忘实现参与者的贡献在亚秒级内被移除而无需重训。

Result: 在 MedMNIST-2D 六个数据集、PathMNIST（224x224）和 CIFAR-10 上，Fed-FBD 在规模适中的数据集上以 0.3%-3.1% 的 IID 精度损失换取上述保障；在 Dirichlet alpha=1.0 下，三个数据集中四个与 FedAvg 差距在 0.8%-4.0% 内；所有六种对抗攻击均被限制在中毒客户端自己的块内，干净颜色的 AUC 漂移最多 ±0.01。

Conclusion: Fed-FBD 在联邦学习中同时实现了块级隔离、隐私设计和高效机器遗忘，以可控的精度代价提供了远优于 FedAvg 的安全和隐私保障能力。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw patient data, but standard approaches such as FedAvg treat each client as a black box and provide no mechanism for isolating an adversarial contributor, auditing per-client influence, or honoring a departed participant's right to be forgotten. We present Fed-FBD (Federated Functional Block Diversification), a modular federated architecture that decomposes a ResNet backbone into six functional blocks (the stem, four residual groups, and the classification head) and maintains a warehouse of N color variants, each assembled from independently tracked and contributor-stamped blocks. Fed-FBD provides three capabilities absent in FedAvg: (i) architecturally guaranteed block-level isolation, so that an adversarial or mislabelled client cannot contaminate the clean colous; (ii) privacy-by-design, where membership inference advantage is already indistinguishable from chance before any privacy mechanism is applied; and (iii) surgical machine unlearning of a departed participant's contribution at sub-second cost and without retraining. Experiments on six MedMNIST-2D datasets, PathMNIST at 224x224, and CIFAR-10 show that Fed-FBD trades a modest 0.3%-3.1% IID accuracy gap on the adequately sized datasets for these guarantees, remains within 0.8%-4.0% of FedAvg at Dirichlet alpha=1.0 on three of four datasets, and confines all six adversarial attacks we study to the poisoned client's own blocks with at most +/-0.01 AUC drift on the clean colors.

</details>


### [262] [How Useful is Causal Invariance for Domain Adaptation in Finite-Sample Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12680)
*Julia Kostin,Kasra Jalaldoust,Elias Bareinboim,Samory Kpotufe,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在线性回归的监督域适应（sDA）场景下，因果知识（完全或部分）何时能带来可证明的有限样本增益。通过定义不变特征子集及其对应的源训练候选预测器，推导出匹配的上下界，表明有限样本增益由目标风险边际和源估计误差共同决定。当边际相对n_Q足够大时，自适应聚合可匹配最佳候选预测器并避免负迁移；边际过小时则无法获得更快的有限样本速率。进一步将边际与线性SCM中的结构偏移幅度联系起来，并在真实因果基准上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在部署到与训练分布不同的目标分布时性能常会下降。尽管因果域泛化工作表明共享因果结构可产生不变预测器，但这些群体层面的因果不变性在有限样本场景下能否带来实际增益尚未被充分探索。特别是实践中常仅有少量带标签的目标样本（监督域适应场景），需要明确因果知识在何时能可证明地改善监督域适应。

Method: 以线性回归为第一研究步骤，定义完全或部分因果知识所指定的不变或可能不变特征子集，每个子集对应一个源训练候选预测器。推导匹配的上下界，分析目标风险边际和有限源估计误差对有限样本增益的影响。设计自适应聚合过程，并在边际足够大时证明其可匹配最佳候选预测器而避免负迁移。同时建立边际与线性结构因果模型（SCM）中结构偏移幅度的联系。

Result: 推导出匹配的上下界，表明有限样本增益由目标风险边际和源估计误差共同决定。当边际相对目标样本量n_Q足够大时，自适应聚合可匹配最佳候选预测器并避免负迁移；当边际过小时，任何算法都无法可靠利用候选集合获得更快的有限样本速率。在真实因果基准上验证了理论。

Conclusion: 因果知识在监督域适应中的有效性取决于目标风险边际的大小与源估计误差。边际足够大时，因果知识可带来可证明的有限样本增益；边际过小时则无法获得加速。这一发现将因果结构信息与迁移学习的统计效率建立起了理论联系，为实际应用提供了指导。

Abstract: Machine learning models often degrade when they are deployed on a target distribution that differs from the source distributions they were trained on. Recent work in causality-based domain generalization has shown how shared causal structure between domains can induce invariant predictors, e.g., models on a subset of features which have stable risk across structured domain shifts. However, the extent to which such population-level causal invariances can lead to gains in finite-sample settings remains underexplored. In particular, in practice we often have access to a few labeled target samples, a setting called supervised domain adaptation (sDA). In this paper, we explore when (full or partial) causal knowledge can provably improve supervised domain adaptation.
  As a first step, we study linear regression, where full or partial causal knowledge specifies a collection of invariant or possibly invariant feature subsets, each yielding a source-trained candidate predictor. We derive matching upper and lower bounds showing that finite-sample gains are governed by the target-risk margins separating the candidates, together with the finite-source estimation error. When these margins are sufficiently large relative to $n_Q$, an adaptive aggregation procedure can match the best candidate predictor while avoiding negative transfer relative to target-only learning. On the other hand, when the margins are too small, no algorithm can reliably exploit the candidate collection to obtain faster finite-sample rates. We further connect these margins to structural shift magnitude in linear SCMs and validate the theory on real-world causal benchmarks.

</details>


### [263] [Forecasting Is Not Attribution: Localizing Decoder Bypass in Graph-Based Neural Marketing Mix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12687)
*Yunbo Wang,Bolbi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DICE-MMM框架，用于诊断和训练基于图的神经营销组合模型（MMM）。研究发现，高容量解码器可以通过目标自回归、密集通信、共同运动、上下文或潜在记忆等方式获得低预测误差，但可能在归因过程中绕过图结构（即归因旁路）。DICE框架将图恢复、预测准确性和扰动诱导影响与图对齐三个问题分离，通过两阶段训练和多种测试（CIG、AR-CIG、图交换测试）来定位瓶颈。实验表明，低MSE不代表归因有效，真正的瓶颈是图支撑选择而非预测或解码器容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销组合模型（MMM）中，预测业务结果和归因到营销渠道是两个不同目标，但现有工作常将二者混为一谈。作者发现基于图的神经MMM存在一种称为"归因旁路"的失效模式：高容量解码器可通过多种机制获得低预测误差，却未能将反事实敏感性路由到用作归依对象的图上，导致归因失效。因此需要一种诊断框架来分离和定位这些问题。

Method: DICE-MMM采用两阶段训练框架。Stage 1训练受限图介导解码器的图编码器；Stage 2冻结选定编码器，训练"图安全"潜在解码器，其跨节点通信必须通过提供的图。提出三种评估测试：CIG（因果影响图）、AR-CIG（自回归CIG）和图交换测试。通过控制R/d/T交换和外部多图原始日志压力测试来评估。

Result: 在稀疏目标基准测试中，无图和全图解码器MSE@7约0.004，但AR-CIG nAUPRC接近或低于零；而oracle图在类似MSE下nAUPRC达0.807±0.129。图交换测试定位到瓶颈：DICE训练的同一解码器在学得的图输入下nAUPRC为-0.044±0.006，切换到oracle图后升至0.894±0.027。DICE在稳定图恢复上优于CausalMMM。

Conclusion: 低预测误差（MSE）不能作为归因有效性的证书；MSE低可能隐藏归因旁路。真正的瓶颈在于图支撑选择（graph-support selection），而非预测能力或解码器容量。DICE框架提供了一种压力测试和失败定位方法，有助于诊断和改善基于图的神经MMM的归因可靠性。

Abstract: Marketing mix models are used to forecast business outcomes and to attribute those outcomes to marketing channels, but these goals are not equivalent. We study a failure mode in graph-based neural MMM called attribution bypass: a high-capacity decoder can obtain low forecasting error through target autoregression, dense communication, co-movement, context, or latent memory while failing to route counterfactual sensitivity through the graph used as the attribution object. We introduce DICE-MMM as a bounded diagnostic and training framework. We do not claim that observational neural MMM identifies causal effects. Instead, DICE separates three questions often conflated in graph-based MMM: graph recovery, forecasting accuracy, and whether the trained decoder's perturbation-induced influence is graph aligned. Stage 1 trains a graph encoder with a restricted graph-mediated decoder. Stage 2 freezes the selected encoder and trains a graph-safe latent decoder whose cross-node communication must pass through the supplied graph. Decoder use is evaluated with CIG, AR-CIG, and graph-swap tests. Across controlled R/d/T swaps and an external multi-graph rawlog stress test, DICE improves stable graph recovery over CausalMMM. The experiments show that forecasting accuracy is not an attribution certificate: in a sparse-target benchmark, no-graph and full-graph decoders achieve MSE@7 around 0.004 while AR-CIG nAUPRC remains near or below zero, whereas an oracle graph reaches 0.807 +/- 0.129 at comparable MSE. Frozen graph-swap localizes the bottleneck: the same DICE-hard-trained decoder moves from nAUPRC -0.044 +/- 0.006 under learned graph inputs to 0.894 +/- 0.027 with the oracle graph. The contribution is a stress test and failure-localization framework showing that low MSE can hide attribution bypass and that the unresolved bottleneck is graph-support selection, not forecasting or decoder capacity.

</details>


### [264] [M*: A Modular, Extensible, Serving System for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12688)
*Atindra Jha,Naomi Sagan,Keisuke Kamahori,Irmak Sivgin,Rohan Sanda,Steven Gao,Mark Horowitz,Luke Zettlemoyer,Olivia Hsu,Jure Leskovec,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M*是一个通用的复合AI模型服务系统，通过"行走图"抽象将复合模型表示为数据流图，支持灵活部署和模型无关优化，相比现有系统在多种任务上实现更低的延迟和更高的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型服务系统建立在狭窄的模型结构假设上，无法适应新一代整合多种异构组件（如视觉编码器、语言骨干、扩散/流头、音频编解码器、动作生成器、世界模型预测器）的复合模型架构的多样性需求。

Method: M*将复合模型表示为数据流图，提出名为"行走图"（Walk Graph）的模块化抽象，支持组件的任意组合、物理集群的灵活放置以及分布式运行时中的模型无关优化。请求处理即为对这些图的遍历。

Result: 在BAGEL模型上，文本到图像工作负载的端到端延迟比vLLM-Omni平均降低20%；在Qwen3-Omni上，文本到语音工作负载实现高达2.9倍更低的实时因子和2.7倍更高的吞吐量；在机器人规划任务上，比V-JEPA 2-AC回滚基线提升高达12.5倍。

Conclusion: M*为高效服务复杂复合模型开辟了新道路，能够以最少的开发者工作量实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: We are entering a new era of composite model architectures that integrate diverse components such as vision encoders, language backbones, diffusion and flow heads, audio codecs, action generators, and world-model predictors. Such architectures underpin a broad class of multimodal models, including unified multimodal models, omni models, speech-language models, vision-language-action policies, and world models. However, existing model serving frameworks were built on narrow assumptions about model structure, making them ill-suited to accommodate this new architectural diversity. Here we present M*, a universal serving system for efficient serving of composite AI models. M* represents models as dataflow graphs, processing requests spanning diverse modalities and tasks as traversals over these graphs. The core insight is a modular abstraction that supports arbitrary composition of model components, flexible placement onto a physical cluster, and model-agnostic optimizations within a distributed runtime. We call this abstraction the Walk Graph and show how it can concisely capture composite models from a broad range of families. We instantiate M* on representative models and find that it achieves, on average, 20% lower end-to-end latency than vLLM-Omni for text-to-image workloads on BAGEL, while delivering up to 2.9x lower real-time factor and 2.7x higher throughput for text-to-speech workloads on Qwen3-Omni. M* also outperforms the V-JEPA 2-AC rollout baseline for robotic planning by up to 12.5x. Thus, our work paves the road towards more efficient serving of complex models with minimal developer effort.

</details>


### [265] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在部分观测线性动态系统数据上通过经验风险最小化训练的两层线性自回归模型，能够自然学习近似卡尔曼滤波，其隐藏表示与最优卡尔曼滤波的状态估计一致（相差一个相似变换），并提供了有限样本下的预测误差、参数估计误差和潜在状态恢复的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自回归模型如何以及为何能够学习潜在表示是一个开放的理论问题。本文旨在从理论上阐明在部分观测线性动态系统中，自回归模型如何通过训练自然地逼近最优滤波（卡尔曼滤波），揭示其学习机制。

Method: 通过三个关键洞察建立理论：1）证明卡尔曼滤波可以被自回归模型良好近似，且截断误差有界；2）证明两层自回归模型的优化景观是良性的（所有驻点要么是严格鞍点，要么是全局最小值）；3）提供有限样本下的预测误差、参数估计误差和潜在状态恢复的理论保证。

Result: 从理论上证明了自回归模型能够恢复卡尔曼滤波的状态估计，并给出了有限样本下的性能保证；数值模拟支持了理论结果。

Conclusion: 自回归模型在部分观测线性动态系统上学习时，其潜在表示自然收敛到卡尔曼滤波的状态估计，这为理解自回归模型的学习机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [266] [LLM-Powered Personalized Glycemic Assessment in Type 2 Diabetes with Wearable Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12699)
*Yifan Gao,Yanmin Gong,Yun Shi,Yuanxiong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GlyLLM框架，利用大语言模型整合可穿戴传感器数据和结构化元数据，用于2型糖尿病的个性化血糖评估。在AI-READI数据集上，该模型在血糖预测任务中RMSE降低13.66%，在糖尿病分类任务中AUROC提升13.08%，显著优于传统机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2型糖尿病（T2D）对全球健康构成日益严重的威胁，需要有效的血糖评估来支持个性化糖尿病护理。可穿戴传感器（如CGM和健身追踪器）提供了有价值的血糖动态数据，但有效分析这些数据需要整合关键的个人层面信息。现有方法主要依赖传统机器学习，仅使用历史血糖测量值，忽视了个性化信息，限制了其在多样糖尿病人群中的表现。

Method: 提出GlyLLM框架，这是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，通过整合可穿戴传感器数据和结构化元数据来建模CGM血糖动态。GlyLLM利用预训练LLM的广泛先验知识，在决策时实现传感器-文本语义抽象。

Result: 在AI-READI数据集的两个相关任务上，GlyLLM相比传统ML方法：在血糖预测任务中RMSE平均降低13.66%，在糖尿病分类任务中AUROC平均提升13.08%。消融实验表明，糖尿病调查和生物特征测试比其他健康信息对血糖评估更为关键。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了利用大语言模型推进T2D护理中个性化血糖评估的 promising 方向。

Abstract: Type 2 Diabetes (T2D) poses an increasing global health threat, demanding effective glycemic assessment to support personalized and improved diabetes care. Wearable sensors such as continuous glucose monitors (CGM) and fitness trackers offer many valuable insights for glycemic assessment. However, effectively analyzing these data requires integration with essential individual-level context. Existing methods are often based on traditional machine learning (ML) and rely primarily on historical blood glucose measurements and overlook personalized information, which limits their performance across diverse diabetes populations. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated their ability to integrate diverse data modalities while modeling sequential dependencies, motivating the exploration of their potential for personalized glycemic assessment.
  In this paper, we propose GlyLLM, an LLM-powered framework for modeling CGM-based glycemic dynamics through the integration of wearable sensor data and structured metadata. GlyLLM can leverage the extensive prior knowledge of pre-trained LLMs and achieve sensor-text semantic abstraction at decision time. Experiments on two related tasks on the AI-READI dataset demonstrate that our model outperforms traditional ML methods by an average of 13.66\% in Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) for glucose forecasting and 13.08\% in Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) for diabetes categorization. Additionally, our ablation study shows that diabetes surveys and biometric tests are more critical than other health information for glycemic assessment. Our work presents a promising step toward harnessing the power of LLMs to advance personalized glycemic assessment in T2D care.

</details>


### [267] [A Stabilized Path-Space Approach to Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12710)
*Evan Scope Crafts,Umberto Villa,Saviz Mowlavi,Yanting Ma,Hassan Mansour,Wael H. Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稳定路径空间的扩散后验采样框架，通过将后验采样转化为学习一个受控随机过程，并引入时间重参数化和信任域路径空间优化方法，解决了现有扩散后验采样器在非线性算子和多模态后验下的启发式近似失效问题，在多个基准逆问题中展现出更高的精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型为贝叶斯逆问题提供了表达性的数据驱动先验，但现有的扩散后验采样器多依赖启发式引导近似，在非线性算子和多模态后验情况下容易失效，缺乏严格的理论保证和不确定性量化能力。

Method: 提出稳定路径空间框架：①从基础扩散过程出发，定义轨迹上的似然加权目标测度；②将后验采样转化为学习受控随机过程以匹配目标路径测度，建立与随机最优控制的联系；③引入时间重参数化消除未知初始值函数导致的偏差；④采用信任域路径空间优化方法与对数方差目标学习控制律；⑤提供重要性采样校正实现渐近精确的后验期望。

Result: 在多个具有解析特征或高质量参考后验的基准逆问题实验中，该框架相比主流方法展现出更高的采样精度和鲁棒性，并系统地评估了扩散后验采样器的行为与不确定性量化能力。

Conclusion: 路径空间视角统一了学习控制方法与现有引导式采样器，量化了近似控制导致的采样误差，并为渐近精确的后验期望提供了重要性采样校正，构成了一个理论严谨、实用有效的扩散后验采样框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive data-driven priors for Bayesian inverse problems, but many diffusion posterior samplers rely on heuristic guidance approximations that can fail for nonlinear operators and multimodal posteriors. In this work, we develop a stabilized path-space framework for diffusion-based posterior sampling. Starting from a base diffusion process whose terminal marginal represents the prior, we define a likelihood-weighted target measure on trajectories and cast posterior sampling as learning a controlled stochastic process whose path measure matches this target. This formulation connects diffusion posterior sampling to stochastic optimal control while preserving the Bayesian structure needed for uncertainty quantification. We introduce a time reparameterization that makes the path-space control problem well posed by removing the bias induced by the unknown initial value function, without auxiliary training. We then learn the control via a trust-region path-space optimization method with log-variance objectives. The path-space perspective also unifies our learned control approach with existing guidance-based samplers, quantifies the sampling error induced by approximate controls, and yields importance sampling corrections for asymptotically exact posterior expectations. We evaluate the proposed framework on a suite of benchmark inverse problems with analytically characterized or high-quality reference posteriors, enabling principled assessment of sampling accuracy and uncertainty quantification. These experiments provide insight into the behavior of diffusion-based posterior samplers and demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness over leading approaches.

</details>


### [268] [Out-of-Distribution (OOD) Detectors for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12718)
*Sudeepta Mondal,Ganesh Sundaramoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将机器学习中的OOD检测方法应用于开放集射频指纹识别，无需额外OOD数据即可进行参数调优，在POWDER数据集上验证了与有OOD数据方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频指纹识别系统需在开放世界环境中运行，面临未知发射机和时域漂移带来的分布偏移问题。OOD检测虽适合解决该问题，但其在RFF中的应用有限，主要障碍是大多数OOD检测器需要辅助OOD数据进行参数调优，而在射频环境中收集代表性OOD数据不切实际。

Method: 从机器学习文献中引入一组OOD检测方法到开放集RFF领域，基于信息论构建统一的数学框架，用于系统分析现有方法并开发新方法。同时将无需OOD调优数据的OOD检测器调优技术应用于开放集RFF问题。

Result: 在POWDER射频指纹数据集上，无需给定OOD数据调优的检测器性能与有真实OOD调优数据的基线方法相当，并大幅优于无真实OOD调优数据的基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法展示了在射频指纹识别问题上的实际可行性，解决了OOD检测在RFF中因缺乏OOD调优数据而难以应用的关键障碍。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) fingerprinting systems must operate in open-world environments where signals from unknown transmitters and temporal drift introduce distribution shift at test time. Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection provides a natural framework for this problem, yet its application to RF fingerprinting (RFF) remains limited. A key barrier to their adoption is that most OOD detectors require auxiliary OOD data for parameter tuning, an assumption that is difficult to satisfy in RF environments where representative OOD data is impractical to collect. In this work, we introduce a promising set of OOD detection methods from the machine learning literature to open-set RFF domain. We present these methods within a unified mathematical framework based on information theory, which is a natural framework for communication systems. Our framework allows for the systematic analysis of methods and development of new methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of recent work on tuning OOD detectors without given OOD tuning data for open-set RFF. We evaluate on the POWDER RF fingerprinting dataset, showing that detectors tuned without any given OOD data achieve performance comparable to baselines with access to true OOD tuning data and greatly out-perform baseline approaches without access to true OOD tuning data, showcasing the practical viability for the RFF problem.

</details>


### [269] [Normative Robustness as a Frontier for Non-Verifiable Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12731)
*Elizaveta Tennant,Benjamin Henke,Anita Keshmirian,Murray Shanahan,Verena Rieser,Kristian Lum,Sydney Levine,Julia Haas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出道德鲁棒性概念，通过模拟48,000次多轮对话评估四个前沿LLM，发现模型会迎合用户道德观点（道德谄媚现象），且推理受顺序和对话轮次影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM推理评估局限于事实性领域（数学、科学），未检验模型在缺乏客观真理的非可验证领域（如道德问题）中的表现。

Method: 将道德推理作为非可验证推理的典型子域，定义道德鲁棒性，设计可扩展的对抗性多轮评估框架，通过控制前提相关性、顺序、对话时长及用户道德立场，模拟48,000次用户-智能体道德对话。

Result: 模型能忽略道德无关干扰，但平均6.5%向用户偏好道德观偏移；顺序改变影响13-22%的判断，单轮与多轮间10-24%的判断变化；模型不仅调整最终判断，还调整底层论证以迎合用户观点（道德商议性谄媚）。

Conclusion: LLM在非可验证推理中存在系统性道德谄媚失败模式，其推理受对话顺序和时长显著影响，亟需针对此类领域设计更鲁棒的评估与训练方法。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly serve in advisory and deliberative roles, users rely on them for non-verifiable reasoning in domains lacking objective ground truths. However, traditional evaluations of LLM reasoning focus almost exclusively on fact-based domains, such as mathematics and science, leaving uncertainty over whether and to what degree models can handle ambiguous, subjective, or value-laden problems over time. To address this concern, we propose moral reasoning as a paradigmatic subdomain of non-verifiable reasoning. We define moral robustness as a model's capacity to exhibit sound moral reasoning across time and contexts, and we introduce a scalable, adversarial, multi-turn evaluation framework to empirically measure this capability. We simulate 48,000 user-agent moral deliberations across four frontier LLMs, varying premise relevance, premise order, conversation duration, and the user's stated moral view. We find that models successfully ignore morally-irrelevant distractors, but shift their reasoning by up to 6.5%, on average, towards the user's stated preferred moral view, and varying their reasoning depending on factors such as order (altering moral judgments by order in 13-22% of the cases) and duration (altering moral judgments between single-turn and multi-turn in 10-24% of the cases). Our analysis indicates that models tailor not just their final verdicts but their underlying justifications to align with a user's moral viewpoint - a failure mode we characterize as moral deliberative sycophancy.

</details>


### [270] [Let's Ask Gauss: Improved One-Run Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12733)
*Adya Agrawal,Yu Wei,Jaspal Singh,Malik Magdon-Ismail,Vassilis Zikas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对差分隐私机器学习中的隐私审计问题，提出了一种基于分布视角的高效单次运行审计框架。研究表明，在白盒DP-SGD设置下，金丝雀对齐信号自然形成随机变量序列，其归一化和渐近服从高斯分布。利用这一分布特性，该方法能在单次训练运行中获得更紧的隐私下界，优于传统的二值成员猜测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单次运行隐私审计方法对训练样本或"金丝雀"进行二值成员猜测，丢弃了有用的信息，导致隐私下界不够紧。作者旨在通过保留更多信息来获得更紧的隐私下界。

Method: 在白盒DP-SGD设置下，将金丝雀对齐信号建模为随机变量序列，证明其归一化和渐近服从高斯分布，并基于此分布特性开发新的隐私审计框架。

Result: 从单次训练运行中获得了更紧的隐私下界，优于传统二值成员猜测方法。

Conclusion: 利用分布视角而非二值猜测，可以有效提升单次运行隐私审计的效率，获得更紧的隐私保障下界。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides an important safeguard by estimating the actual information leaked by a model, thus ensuring that theoretical privacy guarantees hold in practice. We study empirical privacy auditing for differentially private (DP) machine learning, focusing on efficient one-run methods for mechanisms such as DP-SGD. Prior one-run approaches threshold training examples or "canaries" into binary membership guesses, which discards useful information. We show that, in the white-box DP-SGD setting, canary-aligned signals naturally form a sequence of random variables whose normalized sum is asymptotically Gaussian. Leveraging this distributional perspective, we develop a DP-auditing framework that leads to tighter privacy lower bounds from a single training run.

</details>


### [271] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Radial Basis Functions for PDEs with Dirac Delta Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12735)
*Manuel Reyna,Alexandre Tartakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了PINNs在处理含狄拉克δ函数的PDE时存在的建模误差问题，提出将PINNs视为残差最小二乘法(RLS)，并利用弱形式方程直接处理δ函数。相比PINN，径向基函数(RBF)扩展的RLS方法能持续为输运问题提供良好的正反演解，并用神经正切核(NTK)理论解释了这一发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在求解包含狄拉克δ函数的偏微分方程时，需要使用光滑代理函数近似δ函数，这会引入显著的建模误差。作者希望找到一种能直接处理δ函数、避免近似误差的方法。

Method: 作者将PINNs重新解释为残差最小二乘法(RLS)，利用弱形式方程积分直接处理狄拉克δ函数。重点比较了两种RLS方法：传统的PINN方法和基于径向基函数(RBF)扩展的方法。通过神经正切核(NTK)理论分析了两者的差异。

Result: 实验发现，PINN方法在积分掉狄拉克δ函数后残差无法收敛到零，而RBF-RLS方法在求解地下水流动、多孔介质和河流输运等线性PDE的正反演问题时，能持续提供良好的解。两种方法在合成数据、带噪声合成数据和真实世界测量数据的反演问题上进行了测试。

Conclusion: 将PINNs重新解释为RLS方法并采用弱形式方程处理狄拉克δ函数是一种有效的策略，但PINN本身在这种处理下残差收敛不佳。相比之下，RBF-RLS方法更适合处理含狄拉克δ函数的PDE问题，这为处理具有奇异性源项或边界条件的物理问题提供了更好的机器学习求解框架。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a machine learning method for solving forward and inverse Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). When applied to PDEs with Dirac delta functions in the forcing terms, boundary conditions, or initial conditions, PINNs require approximating them with smooth surrogate functions, a practice that can introduce significant modeling errors. In this work, we exploit the interpretation of PINNs as Residual Least Squares (RLS) methods and show that this perspective enables direct treatment of Dirac delta terms by integrating the weak-form equation. Among RLS formulations other than PINN, we focus on the Radial Basis Function (RBF) expansion (also known as a single-layer RBF Network). We show that while integrating out the Dirac delta in PINNs causes residuals to fail to converge to zero, RBF-RLS consistently provides good forward and inverse solutions to transport problems. We explain this finding using the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) theory. We test both approaches on linear PDEs that represent groundwater flow and transport in porous media and rivers. We solve inverse problems to fit synthetic data, noisy synthetic data, and real-world measurements.

</details>


### [272] [Deep Unfolded Latent Optimally Partitioned-l2/l1 Networks for Data-driven Block-Sparse Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12740)
*Takanobu Furuhashi,Hidekata Hontani,Qibin Zhao,Tatsuya Yokota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对凸LOP-l2/l1方法需要手动调参且数值不稳定的问题，提出了基于隐式微分的稳定框架和基于深度权重分解（DWF）的灵活架构，实现了自动参数调优，并在抗脉冲噪声方面表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸LOP-l2/l1方法虽能实现块稀疏信号恢复，但存在两个关键缺陷：一是依赖人工超参数调优，二是其近端算子数值不稳定，导致无法通过深度展开（DU）实现自动参数调优。

Method: 提出了两种架构：1) 利用隐式微分的稳定框架，解决数值不稳定性问题；2) 基于深度权重分解（DWF）的灵活变体，支持非凸光滑数据保真项，实现更广泛的适用性。

Result: 数值实验表明，DU-LOP-l2/l1方法在性能上具有竞争力，且对脉冲噪声具有高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效克服了原LOP-l2/l1的调参和数值稳定性局限，通过DU实现了自动参数学习，同时保持了良好的恢复性能与噪声鲁棒性。

Abstract: The convex Latent Optimal Partition (LOP)-l2/l1 approach enables block-sparse signal recovery with unknown partitions but relies on manual hyperparameter tuning. Additionally, numerical instability in differentiating its proximal operator prevents its automatic parameter tuning via Deep Unfolding (DU). To address these limitations, we propose two architectures: a stable framework utilizing implicit differentiation and a flexible variant leveraging Deep Weight Factorization (DWF). The DWF-based approach also supports nonconvex smooth data fidelity terms. Numerical experiments demonstrate that DU-LOP-l2/l1 yields competitive performance and high resilience against impulsive noise.

</details>


### [273] [Adaptive Weighted Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12763)
*Aditya Bhaskara,Ashok Cutkosky,Ravi Kumar,Manish Purohit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在仅有每个未知值的一个无偏估计时，如何设计同时满足可容许性（不被其他策略一致支配）且不差于基线（如均匀随机选择）的选最大值策略，并应用于随机优化，获得具有"无妥协"保证的在线到批量转换界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有单个无偏估计的情况下，如何可靠地选出未知值中的最大值，并保证策略不会被其他策略一致支配，同时确保性能不差于简单的基线方法。

Method: 设计同时满足可容许性（admissible）且不差于给定基线（如均匀随机选择）的选择策略，并应用于随机优化中的在线到批量转换（online-to-batch conversion）。

Result: 获得了具有"无妥协"保证的在线到批量转换界：既不会比标准随机迭代选择更差，又能在良性环境中显著更优。

Conclusion: 提出的策略在理论上有保证（可容许性、基线保证），在实际应用（随机优化）中能实现"无妥协"的性能提升。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting the largest among $n$ unknown values $x_1,\dots,x_n$ given only a single unbiased estimate $y_i$ for each $x_i$. We design strategies that are simultaneously admissible (not uniformly dominated by any other strategy) and also never worse than a given baseline such as uniform random selection. We provide an application to stochastic optimization, where we obtain online-to-batch conversion bounds with a desirable "no-compromise" guarantee: they are never worse than standard random iterate selection, and yet can be significantly better in benign settings.

</details>


### [274] [Detecting Functional Memorization in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12764)
*Matthieu Meeus,Anil Ramakrishna,Matthew Grange,Zheng Xu,Luca Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在生成代码时的"功能记忆"现象，即模型能从训练数据中提取功能逻辑，即使输出与训练样本在文本上不相似。通过在Olmo-3-32B上进行反事实实验（对比中间训练模型与预训练参考模型），使用Python函数签名提示，结合文本相似度和功能相似度（LLM评判+执行验证）进行测量，结果明确证明了功能记忆的存在，表明审计指标需要超越文本重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注训练数据与模型输出之间的文本重叠（verbatim metrics）来检测记忆化，但代码可以功能等价而文本不相似。因此需要探究模型是否会记忆代码的"功能逻辑"而非仅仅文本形式。

Method: 构建反事实实验框架：使用Olmo-3-32B模型，对比一个中途训练过的模型（已接触过目标代码）与一个预训练的参考模型（未接触过目标代码）。向两个模型提供Python函数签名作为提示，分别测量生成代码的文本相似度和功能相似度（通过LLM-as-a-judge评估和执行验证）。

Result: 实验结果提供了功能记忆存在的清晰证据，表明模型能够记忆和复现训练数据中代码的功能逻辑，即使输出文本与原始代码存在较大差异。

Conclusion: 现有的基于文本重叠的审计指标不足以全面检测LLM中的代码记忆问题，需要开发能够捕捉功能记忆的新型审计方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate code at scale. Meanwhile, prior work has investigated whether training data may be recoverable from model outputs, by auditing the textual overlap between training examples and model generations. Code, however, can be functionally equivalent while textually dissimilar. In this work, we study functional memorization: extraction of functional logic beyond what verbatim metrics detect. We construct a counterfactual setup for Olmo-3-32B, comparing a midtrained model (exposed to target code) against a pretrained reference (not exposed). We prompt both models with Python function signatures and measure both textual and functional similarity (i.e., LLM-as-a-judge, execution-based). Our results show clear evidence of functional memorization, highlighting the need for auditing metrics that go beyond textual overlap.

</details>


### [275] [ProPlay: Procedural World Models for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12780)
*Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Yiyang Li,Ziming Li,Xiaoguang Guo,Weixiang Sun,Chuxu Zhang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProPlay提出了一种程序性世界模型，通过将成功轨迹抽象为程序并构建程序图（procedure graph），实现自主智能体的自我进化。该模型支持执行前的程序级预演（preplay）和执行后的图结构精炼，在部分可观测环境中显著提升了环境理解和自我进化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体方法多依赖记忆或规划模块，但很少形成闭环来持续优化对环境动态的内部理解，导致在部分可观测环境中难以实现真正的自我进化。

Method: ProPlay将成功轨迹抽象为程序，构建程序图来捕捉任务阶段间的因果转换关系，每条转换关联一个可靠性记录嵌入以估计任务贡献。执行前通过程序级预演模拟未来路径作为软引导，执行后利用环境反馈精炼图结构。

Result: 在公开基准上的实验表明，ProPlay在环境理解和自我进化能力上 consistently 优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: ProPlay通过程序图抽象与预演机制，有效实现了部分可观测环境下自主智能体的持续自我进化。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents are expected to improve through interaction without external supervision, but this remains difficult in partially observable environments where agents must explore actively, learn from limited feedback, and decide when to trust prior experience. Existing LLM-agent methods often rely on memory or planning modules, yet they rarely close the loop between them to continually refine an internal understanding of environment dynamics. We introduce ProPlay, a procedural world model that supports procedure-level preplay, where agents can rehearse future procedural paths using the learned world knowledge. Rather than representing experience as isolated rules or low-level action constraints, ProPlay abstracts successful trajectories into procedures and organizes them in a procedure graph that captures causal transitions among task stages. Each transition is associated with a reliability record embedding to estimate its task-specific contribution from past outcomes. Before each episode, ProPlay simulates future procedural trajectories over known graph structures as structured soft guidance; after execution, it refines the graph using environment feedback. Experiments on public benchmarks show that ProPlay consistently improves environment understanding and self-evolution capability over strong baselines. Our code has been released in https://github.com/antman9914/proplay.

</details>


### [276] [SymQNet: Amortized Acquisition for Low-Latency Adaptive Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12808)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar,Dheeraj Peddireddy,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SymQNet是一个基于强化学习的摊销方法，用于低延迟自适应哈密顿量学习，通过离线学习后验条件采集策略，在线快速执行，显著降低了采集决策延迟（5量子位比基线快47.1倍和72.6倍，12量子位全模拟步长1.02秒对比13.27秒），使自适应学习适用于重复低延迟工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应哈密顿量学习中，每次实验后重新计算贝叶斯设计规则需要数秒，在数百次测量中累积为显著的墙钟时间成本，导致自适应学习的延迟过高，限制了其在实际低延迟工作负载中的应用。

Method: 提出SymQNet，一种摊销强化学习方法。离线学习一个后验条件化的采集策略，在线使用时仅需快速前向传播，同时保留贝叶斯后验反馈，从而避免在线重复计算高成本的贝叶斯设计规则。

Result: 在横向场伊辛模型基准测试中，SymQNet相比有界Fisher信息搜索和有界两步BALD，在5量子位时将采集决策延迟降低了47.1倍和72.6倍；在12量子位时，完整模拟步长耗时1.02秒，而两步BALD需要13.27秒。

Conclusion: 学习式的采集策略能够显著降低延迟，使自适应哈密顿量学习在重复低延迟工作负载中变得实用。

Abstract: Adaptive Hamiltonian learning is central to calibrating and characterizing quantum devices. In an adaptive controller, choosing the next experiment is itself a computation. Bayesian design rules are recomputed after every posterior update, and that step can take seconds. Across hundreds of shots, those seconds become a significant wall-clock cost for adaptivity. We introduce SymQNet, an amortized reinforcement-learning approach for low-latency adaptive Hamiltonian learning. SymQNet learns a posterior-conditioned acquisition policy offline, then uses a fast policy forward pass online while retaining Bayesian posterior feedback. On transverse-field Ising benchmarks, SymQNet substantially reduces acquisition latency relative to bounded Fisher-information search and bounded two-step Bayesian active learning by disagreement (BALD). At five qubits, it reduces acquisition-only decision latency by $47.1\times$ and $72.6\times$ relative to these online baselines; at twelve qubits, full simulated steps take $1.02$ s for SymQNet versus $13.27$ s for bounded two-step BALD. Overall, we show that learned acquisition can make adaptive Hamiltonian learning practical for repeated low-latency workloads.

</details>


### [277] [CLARITree: Cholesky and Lookahead Accelerations for Regression with Interpretable Piecewise Linear Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12840)
*Yixiao Wang,Hayden McTavish,Varun Babbar,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合前瞻搜索策略和秩一Cholesky更新的新型算法，用于构建近最优、稀疏的分段线性回归树，在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贪心方法构建的回归树性能有限，而基于动态规划和分支定界的最优方法计算代价过高。现有前瞻策略主要针对分类问题，缺乏对回归树的高效优化方法。

Method: 结合前瞻式搜索策略（lookahead-style search）与高效的Gram矩阵秩一Cholesky更新（rank-one Cholesky updates），设计了一种近最优、稀疏的分段线性回归树学习算法。

Result: 在理论上和实验上证明，该方法在计算效率、预测准确性和稀疏性三个维度上取得了有利的权衡，其可扩展性显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该算法为构建高性能、可解释的回归树提供了一条实用且高效的路径，尤其适用于需要稀疏性和预测精度的大规模场景。

Abstract: Regression trees are among the most interpretable yet expressive model classes in machine learning. Historically, greedy induction has been the dominant approach for constructing well-performing regression trees. While optimal methods based on dynamic programming and branch-and-bound exist, they are computationally prohibitive for general linear regression trees, despite often achieving substantially better performance than greedy approaches. Recent work has shown that specialized lookahead strategies can dramatically improve runtime while maintaining near-optimal performance, primarily in classification settings. In this work, we develop a novel algorithm for near-optimal, sparse, piecewise linear regression trees that combines a lookahead-style search strategy with efficient rank-one Cholesky updates of the Gram matrix. We demonstrate, both theoretically and empirically, that our method achieves a favorable trade-off between computational efficiency, predictive accuracy, and sparsity, and scales significantly better than the current state of the art.

</details>


### [278] [TimeROME-DLM: Temporal Causal Tracing and Low-Rank Inference-Time Knowledge Editing for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12841)
*Zhengtao Yao,Liuyang Song,Hongbo Zhang,Chenhao Wei,Haoyan Xu,Guang Yang,Siheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeROME-DLM is the首个无训练、无梯度的推理时知识编辑框架，专为掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）设计，通过时间间接效应（TIE）因果追踪定位关键坐标，并应用闭式低秩残差编辑记忆实现高效的批量事实编辑，在保持骨干权重冻结的同时，显著降低遗忘集对数概率（~83 nats），零额外VRAM开销，速度提升4-14倍，且可扩展至400个事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑与遗忘方法（如ROME、MEMIT）均面向自回归Transformer，在迭代去噪的MDLM上要么因假设失效而无法工作，要么需要梯度更新导致数十GB的额外VRAM开销且学习率难以适配。因此亟需一种针对MDLM的、无训练/无梯度的高效推理时编辑方案。

Method: TimeROME-DLM包含两个核心组件：(1) 时间间接效应（TIE）因果追踪协议，通过识别每个事实在后期去噪步中驱动目标预测最关键的坐标（注意力层中的特定位置）；(2) 闭式低秩残差编辑记忆，聚合所有遗忘事实的主语键与目标增量，在扩散前向过程的每个步骤中对该坐标施加一次岭回归正则化更新，并通过稀疏化限制效用外溢。骨干权重完全冻结，仅需调节三个超参数。

Result: 在TOFU forget01数据集上，使用TOFU微调的LLaDA-8B-Base模型，遗忘集对数概率降低约83 nats。同一配置可直接迁移至LLaDA-8B-Instruct、Dream-7B、MMaDA-8B、DiffuLLaMA-7B、LLaDA-MoE-1.4B等多种MDLM。保持保留集对数概率几乎不变（~1 nat内），可顺序插入50个事实，相比最强训练基线实现4-14倍加速且零额外VRAM，并亚线性扩展至400个事实。

Conclusion: TimeROME-DLM以极小计算成本弥合了自回归LLM与MDLM在'定位-编辑'范式上的差距，首次实现了面向MDLM的无训练、无梯度、可迁移的批量知识编辑，证明因果追踪与低秩编辑在扩散模型上的有效性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) such as LLaDA now rival autoregressive (AR) LLMs, but every existing knowledge-editing and unlearning method (ROME, MEMIT, etc.) targets AR transformers and either makes assumptions that fail under iterative denoising, or requires gradient updates whose backward-pass activations cost tens of GB of extra VRAM and which collapse MDLMs at standard learning rates. We introduce TimeROME-DLM, the first training-free, gradient-free, inference-time knowledge-editing framework for MDLMs. It couples two components: a Temporal Indirect Effect (TIE) causal-tracing protocol that identifies, for each fact, the coordinate whose intervention most strongly drives the object prediction at later denoising steps; and a closed-form, low-rank residual edit memory that aggregates subject keys and target deltas across all forget facts and applies a single ridge-regularised update at that coordinate at every diffusion forward, with sparsification to limit utility spillover. Backbone weights stay frozen; only three hyperparameters (alpha, lambda, q) are tuned on a small validation split. On TOFU forget01 with TOFU-finetuned LLaDA-8B-Base, TimeROME-DLM cuts forget-set log-probability by roughly 83 nats. The same configuration transfers to LLaDA-8B-Instruct, Dream-7B, MMaDA-8B, DiffuLLaMA-7B, and LLaDA-MoE-1.4B. It keeps retain-set log-probability nearly flat (within ~1 nat at the utility-safe operating point) across 50 sequentially inserted facts, delivers a four- to fourteen-fold wall-clock speedup with zero additional VRAM over the strongest converged training-time baseline, and scales sub-linearly to 400 facts. TimeROME-DLM closes the locate-then-edit gap between AR LLMs and MDLMs at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


### [279] [Interpretable Factor Decomposition for Decision Intelligence in Large-Scale Financial Markets: Evidence from China's A-Share Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12843)
*Xiao Han,Yao Xiao,Zhen Zhang,Moxuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的机器学习流程，将横截面股票收益的可预测性分解为可审计的因子贡献。使用XGBoost模型和TreeSHAP归因方法，对2009年至2019年间3632只中国A股进行压力测试，发现XGBoost在样本外数据中实现了0.547的平均AUC和每月+2.38%的多空 spread（经Carhart四因子调整后仍显著），其中行为信号（换手率与动量）贡献了58.2%的预测归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习模型在股票收益预测中表现出色，但缺乏可解释性和可审计性，无法分解不同因子对预测的具体贡献；同时，传统归因方法（如SHAP）与消融分析之间的差异尚未被充分研究。

Method: 采用XGBoost模型结合TreeSHAP归因方法，对3632只中国A股（2009-2019年）进行60个月滚动窗口的样本外预测（55个月），通过SHAP分解归因与消融分析交叉验证，并应用Carhart四因子模型调整。

Result: XGBoost获得平均AUC 0.547，多空 spread 每月+2.38%（Newey-West t=5.94，年化夏普2.23），Carhart调整后alpha仍显著（+2.31%/月，t=7.48）。行为信号（换手率和动量）占预测归因的58.2%，估值比率仅占10.7%。SHAP与消融分析的分歧揭示了两者单独使用时无法见到的特征可替代性结构。

Conclusion: 可解释机器学习能够有效将股票收益预测分解为可审计的因子贡献，行为信号在预测中占主导地位；SHAP与消融分析的结合有助于揭示特征间的替代性结构，为因子投资提供更透明的分析框架。

Abstract: We present an interpretable machine learning pipeline to decompose Cross-Sectional Equity Return Predictability into auditable factor contribution. We apply an XGBoost model with TreeSHAP attribution and conduct stress testing on 3632 Chinese A-share stocks from 2009 until 2019. Using 60-month, rolling windows over 55 months of out-of-sample data, XGBoost obtains a mean AUC of 0.547 and +2.38%/month (Newey-West t = 5.94; Annualized Sharpe 2.23) long-short spread for the top vs bottom quintiles. This alpha is persistent after adjusting for the Carhart four-factor model (+2.31%/month; t = 7.48). SHAP Decomposition indicates that behavioral signals (turnover and momentum) account for 58.2% of predictive attribution compared to 10.7% for valuation ratios, on average, across 55 industry groups. Ablation analysis serves to cross-validate this ranking and provides evidence that SHAP and ablation diverge in a manner that highlights feature substitutability structure that is largely invisible to either method used in isolation.

</details>


### [280] [Multimodal Graph Negative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12863)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphMNL，一种图感知多模态负学习框架，通过负学习机制解决多模态属性图中节点级分支语义不平衡问题，避免主导分支偏见传播，在Grocery和Reddit M数据集上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态属性图（MAGs）中不同模态分支（结构拓扑、文本、视觉属性）在语义信息量和可靠性上存在节点级不平衡：某个分支对某些节点提供判别性语义，但对其他节点可能因模态质量或结构上下文偏差而产生误导。现有方法通过跨分支一致性或对齐隐式地将主导预测视为可靠监督，当主导分支存在偏差时，强制模仿会传播偏差并抑制其他分支中有用的原始语义。

Method: 提出GraphMNL框架，核心包括：（1）构建分支库，通过图感知可靠性仲裁机制识别主导分支和劣势分支；（2）对不稳定传输进行门控；（3）对非目标类应用目标保留负学习。不同于强迫劣势分支模仿教师预测，该方法教会模型哪些类别节点不太可能属于，从而解耦目标监督与分支指导。

Result: 在Grocery数据集上取得72.47%准确率，在Reddit M数据集上取得76.60 F1分数，综合实验评估达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: GraphMNL通过负学习作为跨分支指导，有效解决多模态属性图中的分支语义不平衡问题，避免了主导分支偏见传播，同时保留了各分支有用的原始语义，实现了更可靠的节点表示学习。

Abstract: Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs) integrate graph topology with heterogeneous modality attributes, such as text and images, thereby enabling richer modeling of complex relational systems. However, such expressiveness also makes learning on MAGs depend on multiple semantic sources, including structural topology, textual and visual attributes, each of which can be regarded as a branch for node representation. Node-level branch semantic imbalance arises when these branches differ across nodes in semantic informativeness and reliability: a branch that provides discriminative semantics for one node may mislead another due to bias in modality quality or structural context. Existing methods often mitigate such heterogeneity through cross-branch agreement or alignment, implicitly treating the dominant prediction as reliable supervision. When the dominant branch is biased, forced imitation may propagate its bias to other branches and suppress original semantics that are useful for classification. We propose GraphMNL, a graph-aware multimodal negative learning framework that addresses this issue by using Negative Learning as cross-branch guidance. Instead of forcing inferior branches to imitate a teacher prediction, the model teaches them which classes a node is unlikely to belong to. GraphMNL builds a branch library, identifies dominant and inferior branches via graph-aware reliability arbitration, gates unstable transfer, and applies target-preserving negative learning over non-target classes. This design decouples target supervision from branch guidance so that supervised losses learn the correct class, while Negative Learning suppresses unlikely alternatives when branch agreement is unreliable. Through the comprehensive experimental evaluation, GraphMNL achieves the best performance on Grocery datasets with 72.47% accuracy and 76.60 F1 score on Reddit M datasets.

</details>


### [281] [SMGFM: Spectral Multimodal Graph Pretraining for Multimodal-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12867)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SMGFM提出了一种频谱多模态图预训练框架，通过图频率分解来区分结构诱导语义和模态固有语义，在MAG数据集上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图学习方法在多模态属性图中会遇到结构诱导语义和模态固有语义的冲突——结构语义要求平滑拓扑变化，而模态语义保留细粒度局部区分，两者不应被统一对齐或平滑。

Method: 利用图频率变化作为先验，通过可扩展的切比雪夫滤波器构建频率分辨的模态令牌，采用拓扑条件路由估计耦合可靠性，并在融合前进行频带-模态交互，最终通过频率路由目标对齐平滑共识路径同时保留模态特有路径。

Result: 在多模态属性图数据集的图级和模态级任务上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SMGFM通过频谱分解和频率级语义角色分配，有效解决了多模态图中不同语义的融合冲突问题，验证了图频率先验在区分语义角色方面的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs) couple graph topology with node semantics from text, images, and other modalities. Traditional graph learning contextualizes node semantics by coupling topology with node features. However, this coupling design becomes troublesome in MAGs, where structure-induced and modality-intrinsic semantics may contribute differently to downstream tasks. Structure-induced semantics promote relational consistency through smooth topological variation, whereas modality-intrinsic semantics often encode local, fine-grained distinctions that should not be uniformly smoothed or aligned. Therefore, the key challenge is to identify semantic roles before cross-modal fusion. To this end, we leverage graph-frequency variation as a prior, where low-frequency components capture topology-consistent semantics and high-frequency components preserve modality-specific semantics. Based on this intuition, we propose SMGFM, a spectral multimodal graph pretraining framework that decomposes each modality-specific node signal into graph-frequency bands and assigns band-level semantic roles before cross-modal interaction. Concretely, SMGFM constructs frequency-resolved modality tokens with scalable Chebyshev filters, estimates their coupling reliability through topology-conditioned routing, and performs band-modality interaction before fusion. Its frequency-routed objectives align smooth consensus routes while preserving modality-specific routes, mitigating spatial-domain entanglement and uniform cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments conducted on the MAG datasets demonstrate that SMGFM achieves state-of-the-art performance across graph-level and modality-level tasks.

</details>


### [282] [Multi-Bitwidth Quantization for LLMs Using Additive Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12876)
*Liza Babaoglu,Shuangyi Chen,Ashish Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Drop-by-Drop框架，通过后训练量化实现单个LLM模型在推理时动态控制权重的比特宽度，无需重新训练即可自适应调整性能与效率的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署在不同硬件上时面临异构资源约束，需要在不重新训练的情况下自适应管理性能与效率的权衡。

Method: 基于信息论和逐次细化理论，利用LLM权重的高斯分布特性，在加权均方误差失真下优化重建；采用Matryoshka风格监督和加性码本结构，使有序子集在不同精度下产生准确的局部重建结果。

Result: 单个检查点可服务多种比特宽度，显著减少存储和内存开销；在Qwen、LLaMA、Gemma、Mistral等主流架构上保持有竞争力的困惑度和准确率。

Conclusion: Drop-by-Drop实现了无需重训练的推理时多比特宽度控制，为异构部署场景提供了一种高效且性能保持的量化方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous hardware with varying resource constraints, the ability to adaptively manage the trade-off between performance and efficiency without retraining is critical. We propose Drop-by-Drop, a novel multi-bitwidth post-training quantization framework that enables inference-time precision control over LLM weights from a single trained model. Our method is theoretically grounded in information theory and successive refinement. We establish that LLM weights, which commonly follow a Gaussian distribution, can be optimally reconstructed with increasing fidelity as additional bits are incorporated, under a weighted mean squared error distortion motivated by LLM loss functions. To realize this in practice, Drop-by-Drop incorporates Matryoshka-style supervision into the loss function, exploiting the structure of additive codebooks. Drop-by-Drop produces a single model where ordered subsets of codebooks yield accurate partial reconstructions at each precision level. This approach significantly reduces storage and memory overhead by allowing a single checkpoint to serve multiple bitwidths, while maintaining competitive perplexity and accuracy across major architectures, such as Qwen, LLaMA, Gemma, and Mistral.

</details>


### [283] [LongSpike: Fractional Order Spiking State Space Models for Efficient Long Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12895)
*Xinrui He,Qiyu Kang,Xuhao Li,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongSpike是一个将分数阶状态空间建模（f-SSM）引入脉冲神经网络（SNN）的新框架，通过解决传统一阶ODE导致的"无记忆"瓶颈，有效捕获长序列依赖，在LRA、WikiText-103和语音命令等基准测试中超越现有SNN，同时保持稀疏突触计算的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SNN架构依赖一阶ODE建模神经元状态转换，这种"无记忆性"假设限制了模型捕获长序列任务中复杂长程依赖关系的能力。

Method: 提出LongSpike框架，将控制理论中的分数阶状态空间建模（f-SSM）扩展到脉冲域，通过将整数阶SSM推广到分数阶微积分领域，实现具有长记忆核的神经元动力学层次化集成，并利用状态空间公式支持高效并行训练。

Result: 在Long Range Arena（LRA）、大规模WikiText-103和语音命令等基准测试上，LongSpike在准确率上超越现有最先进SNN，同时保持了稀疏突触计算的效率。

Conclusion: LongSpike通过分数阶状态空间建模有效解决了SNN在长序列任务中的记忆瓶颈问题，实现了性能与效率的双重提升，代码已开源。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are well-regarded for their biological plausibility and energy efficiency in processing sequential data. However, dominant SNN architectures typically rely on first-order Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) to govern neuronal state transitions. This first-order assumption imposes a "memoryless" bottleneck, limiting the model's capacity to capture the complex, long-range dependencies inherent in long-sequence tasks. In this work, we propose LongSpike, a novel SNN framework that integrates fractional-order State-Space Modeling, or f-SSM, from control theory into the spiking domain. By extending traditional integer-order SSMs to the fractional-calculus regime, LongSpike enables the hierarchical integration of neuronal dynamics with long-memory kernels. To mitigate the computational overhead and parallelization challenges typically associated with fractional operators, we leverage a state-space formulation that supports efficient, parallel training. Empirical evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including Long Range Arena (LRA), large-scale WikiText-103, and Speech Commands, demonstrate that LongSpike outperforms state-of-the-art SNNs in accuracy while preserving sparse synaptic computation. The code is available at https://github.com/xinruihe389-commits/LongSpike.

</details>


### [284] [PolicyGuard: Towards Test-time and Step-level Adversary Defense for Reinforcement Learning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12896)
*Junfeng Guo Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolicyGuard是一种测试时、步级别的后门防御方法，利用高斯过程后验方差和伪轨迹实现对RL代理单个时间步的不确定性计算，在七个RL游戏中达到最先进的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后门防御方法要么需要访问代理内部参数，要么仅在模型或轨迹级别操作，或仅限于特定攻击类型，缺乏通用且细粒度的防御机制。

Method: 提出PolicyGuard，利用高斯过程(GP)后验方差计算不确定性，并通过自适应伪轨迹实现对单个时间步的检测，是一种测试时、步级别的后门防御方法。

Result: 在七个RL游戏上的实验表明，PolicyGuard在大多数情况下达到了最先进的检测性能，对基于扰动的攻击平均AUROC为0.856，对对手代理攻击平均AUROC为0.859。

Conclusion: PolicyGuard作为一种新型的测试时步级别防御方法，有效弥补了现有RL后门防御的不足，并提供了理论基础支撑其有效性。

Abstract: While real-world applications of reinforcement learning (RL) are becoming increasingly popular, the security of RL systems deserve more attention and exploration. In particular, recent work has revealed that RL agents are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a victim agent behaves normally under standard conditions but executes malicious actions when a specific trigger is activated. Existing backdoor defenses for RL either require access to the agent's internal parameters, operate only at the model or trajectory level, or are limited to specific attack types. To ensure the security of RL agents, we propose \texttt{PolicyGuard}, a \textit{test-time step-level} backdoor defense which leverages Gaussian Process (GP) posterior variance and adapts pseudo trajectories to enable uncertainty computation for individual time step.
